Appendix 0.B
Subtyping Properties

Lemma 29

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}
.

Proof

By the definition of association (
Definition

9
),
the definition of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
(
Definition

7
), and
the transitivity of subtyping.

Lemma 30

Assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
with:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}\,\middle|\,{\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\!\in\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}}\right\}}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof

Since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
, by applying and inverting
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\&}
]
(and
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
when necessary), and also using
Lemma

16
, we have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
,
\exists k:k\in I
,
k\in J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}
.
Furthermore, by
Lemmas

14
and
15
,
it also holds that for all
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
with

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}
.
Observe that by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}
where

I\subseteq I_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
and
\forall i\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{i}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}
where

J_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\subseteq J
and
\forall j\in J_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}
.
Now we apply
Lemma

23
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}}\right)}
, to get

I\subseteq I_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\subseteq J_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\subseteq J
, and

\forall i\in I_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{i}}
.
Consequently, we have
k\in I_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{k}}
, which follows that there exists

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime\prime}_{k}}
, and
for all
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}
with

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}
.

We are left to show that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
, which is straightforward from

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime\prime\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}}\right)}
, and
for all
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
with

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}]}}\right)}
.

Lemma 31

Assume that
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}:\exists{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
with:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}\,\middle|\,{\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\!\in\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}}\right\}}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof

Apply
Definition

7
and
Lemmas

20
and
30
.

Proposition 3

Assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{1}}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
, with:

\forall i\in 1...n:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{i}}\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}\,\middle|\,{\,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\!\in\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}}\right\}}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{1}}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof

By induction on the number of transitions
n
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{1}}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.
The base case (
n=0
transitions) is immediate: we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
,
hence we conclude by taking
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
.
In the inductive case with
n=m+1
transitions,
there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}_{0}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}_{1}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{m}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}_{0}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.
By the induction hypothesis, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{1}}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{m}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}_{0}}
. Hence, by
Lemma

30
, there exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}

such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
. Therefore, we have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{1}}}}}\cdots\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
,
which is the thesis.

Appendix 0.C
Type System Properties

Lemma 32
(Narrowing)

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
.

Proof

By induction on the derivation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
,
we obtain a derivation that concludes
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}

by inserting (possibly vacuous) instances of rule
[T-Sub]
(
Figure

7
).

Lemma 33
(Substitution)

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}
, with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
defined. Then,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}}\right\}}}}
.

Proof

Minor adaptation of
[
4
, Lemma 5]
.

Lemma 34
(Typing Inversion)

Assume

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
. Then:

(1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
implies
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
;

(2)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{def}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}}}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{n}}}})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
implies:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{n}}}}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{1}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\ldots\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{n}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{n}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
, and

(ii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{n}}}}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
;

(3)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}\!\left\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d_{n}}}\right\rangle}
implies:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mathbin{\!:\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{n}}}}
, and

(ii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{0}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\ldots\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{n}}}
, and

(iii)

\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{0}}})
, and

(iv)

\forall i\in 1\ldots n:\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d_{i}}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
;

(4)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}}\right){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
implies:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\not\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
, and

(ii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}
for some
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
, and

(iii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
;

(5)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{2}}
implies:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}
such that

(ii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}}}
and

(iii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{2}}}}
;

(6)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\&\!}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}}({y_{i}})\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}
implies:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{0}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}
such that

(ii)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}}}
and

(iii)

;

(7)

Lemma 35
(Subject Congruence)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

.

See
1

Proof

From the hypothesis
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
,
we know that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{0}}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\!\to\!\cdots\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
(for some
n
).
The proof proceeds by induction on
n
.

The base case for n=0 is straightforward: we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
, thus
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
is well-typed. Furthermore, since the term
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{err}}}
is not typeable,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
cannot contain such a term.

In the inductive case for n = m+1, we already know (by the induction hypothesis) that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{m}}
is well-typed. By applying
Theorem

4.1
, we can conclude that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{m+1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
is also well-typed and does not contain any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{err}}}
subterms.

Appendix 0.E
Proofs for Session Fidelity and Process Properties

See
4.2

Proof

The proof structure is similar to Thm. 5.4 in
[
19
]
: by
induction on the derivation of the reduction of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
,
we infer the contents of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
and then the shape of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
and its
sub-processes
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
, showing that they can mimic the reduction of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
. Most cases hold by applying the induction hypothesis.

•

Case
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
:
in this case, the process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
playing role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
is a selection on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}
towards
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
(possibly within a process definition);
while the process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}
playing role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
is a branching
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}]}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
(possibly within a process definition).
Therefore, by
[R-
\oplus\&
]
in
Figure

2
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
can correspondingly reduce to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
by transmitting
either a basic value
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}
or a channel endpoint
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}^{\prime}}}]}

from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
(possibly after a finite number of transitions under rule
[R-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}
]
). The resulting continuation process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

is typed by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.
The assertion that there exists

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}

follows from

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
and
Theorem

3.2
.

Proposition

4
below says that if a process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
satisfies the assumptions of session fidelity (
Theorem

4.2
)
then all its reductums will satisfy such assumptions, too. This means that if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
enjoys session fidelity, then all its reductums enjoy session fidelity, too.

Proposition 4

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
,  and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}=\bigcup_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
such that, for each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
.  Further, assume that each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}

is either
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
(up to
\equiv
), or only plays
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
. Then,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

implies
\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{\!*}}_{\!\!\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
,  with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}\equiv\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
,  and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}=\bigcup_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
such that, for each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
,
we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
;  furthermore, each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}

is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
(up to
\equiv
),
or only plays
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
.

Proof

Straightforward from the proof of
Theorem

4.1
, which accounts for all possible transitions from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
, and in all cases yields the desired properties for its typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.

Lemma 36
(Process Deadlock-Freedom)

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}=\bigcup_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
such that for each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
.
Further, assume that each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}

is either
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
(up to
\equiv
), or only plays
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
. Then,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is deadlock-free.

Proof

By the assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and
Lemma

27
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
-deadlock-free.

Consider any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}\!\not{\!\!\!\to}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{0}}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\!\to\!\cdots\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}\!\not{\!\!\!\to}
(for some
n
)
with each reduction
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i+1}}
(
i\!\in\!0...n\!-\!1
).
By
Proposition

4
, we know that each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}
is well-typed
and its typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}
is such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{\!*}}_{\!\!\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}
;
moreover,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}
satisfies the single-session requirements of
Theorem

4.2
.
Now observe that, since the process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}\!\not{\!\!\!\to}
cannot reduce further, by the contrapositive of
Theorem

4.2
,
we obtain
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{n}}}\!\!\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}
.
Furthermore, since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
-deadlock-free,
by
Definition

11
,
we have

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}\!\in\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{n}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}
.
Therefore, by
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
]
,
we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
,
which (by
Definition

15
(1)
) is the thesis.

Lemma 37
(Process Liveness)

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Pi_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}=\bigcup_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in I}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
such that for each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\!}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
.
Further, assume that each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}

is either
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
(up to
\equiv
), or only plays
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
. Then,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is live.

Proof

By the assumption
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and
Lemma

28
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
-live.

The proof proceeds by contradiction: assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is
not
live.
Since (by hypothesis) each parallel component of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
only plays one role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
,
this means that there are
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{0}}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\!\to\!\cdots\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}\!\equiv\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}}\!\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}\right]
where either:

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}w}}\rangle\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}}}}
(for some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}w},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}
), and
\not\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}^{\prime}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}\!\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}^{\prime}}}\!\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}}\right]
. By
Proposition

4
, we know that each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}
is well-typed
and its typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}
is such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{\!*}}_{\!\!\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}
;
moreover, each
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}
satisfies the single-session requirements of
Theorem

4.2
.
Therefore,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
satisfies the single-session requirements of
Theorem

4.2
,
and is typed by some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{\!*}}_{\!\!\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.
Hence, by inversion of typing,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}
is typed by some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}
(part of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
)
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}\right)}
is a (possibly recursive) internal choice
towards
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, including a choice
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}
(where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}
types the message payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}w}
).
Therefore, we have
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\!\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}:{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}}}}
.
Now, recall that (for the sake of the proof by contradiction) we are assuming that
no sequence of reductions of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
can fire the top-level selection of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}
;
this means that no parallel component of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
ever exposes an external choice by role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}

including message label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}}
; correspondingly, there is at least
one fair path beginning with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}

(yielded by
Theorem

4.1
) that never fires a transmission label

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}}
(for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}
).
But then, such a fair path starting from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
is not live, hence
(by
Definition

13
) we obtain that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is
not
live, a desired contradiction;

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}]}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\&\!}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}}({x_{i}})\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{i}}}}\}_{i\in I}}
(for some
I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}_{\mathnormal{i}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{i}}
), and

\not\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}^{\prime}},k\!\in\!I,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}w}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}^{\prime}}}\!\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{k}}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}w}}}{{x_{k}}}}\right\}}}\right]
. The proof is similar to the previous case, and reaches a similar contradiction.

In summary, we have shown
that assuming
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is not live leads to a contradiction.
Consequently, we can conclude that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is live.

See
4.3

Proof

Apply
Lemmas

36
and
37
.
